Time Model Statistics

  • DB Time represents total time in user calls
  • DB CPU represents CPU time of foreground processes
  • Total CPU Time represents foreground and background processes
  • Statistics including the word "background" measure background process time, therefore do not contribute to the DB time statistic
  • Ordered by % of DB time in descending order, followed by Statistic Name

0ub10pbbdtrxhSELECT /*+ ALL_ROWS */ dbms_ash_internal.get_blk_str(ash.blocking_session, ash.blocking_session_serial#, ash.blocking_inst_id) as sess, ash.dim1_percentage, ash.event, ash.dim12_percentage, dbms_ash_internal.get_blksid_user( ash.blocking_session, ash.blocking_session_serial#, ash.blocking_inst_id), dbms_ash_internal.get_blksid_program( ash.blocking_session, ash.blocking_session_serial#, ash.blocking_inst_id, 30), dbms_ash_internal.get_blksid_active( ash.blocking_session, ash.blocking_session_serial#, ash.blocking_inst_id, 18), dbms_ash_internal.get_blksid_xids( ash.blocking_session, ash.blocking_session_serial#, ash.blocking_inst_id) FROM ( SELECT d12aa_ash.* FROM ( SELECT d12gb_ash.*, (dim1_count*100/:ash_num_rows) as dim1_percentage, dense_rank() over (order by dim1_count desc, blocking_session, blocking_session_serial#, blocking_inst_id ) as dim1_rank FROM ( SELECT blocking_session, blocking_session_serial#, blocking_inst_id , event , count(*) as dim12_count, (count(*) * 100 / :ash_num_rows) as dim12_percentage, rank() over (partition by blocking_session, blocking_session_serial#, blocking_inst_id order by count(*) desc, event ) as dim12_rank, sum(count(*)) over (partition by blocking_session, blocking_session_serial#, blocking_inst_id) as dim1_count FROM ( SELECT ash.* FROM (SELECT a.dbid, a.instance_number, a.snap_id, nvl(a.con_id, -1) as con_id, a.con_dbid, nvl(p.pdb_name, 'UNKNOWN DBID ' || a.con_dbid) as pdb_name, a.sample_id, a.sample_time, a.session_id, a.session_serial#, a.session_type, a.session_state, a.flags, a.user_id, a.sql_id, a.sql_opcode, a.sql_opname, a.top_level_sql_id, a.sql_child_number, a.sql_plan_hash_value, nvl(a.sql_full_plan_hash_value, a.sql_plan_hash_value) as full_plan_hash_value, a.sql_plan_line_id, a.sql_plan_operation, a.sql_plan_options, a.sql_exec_id, a.force_matching_signature, a.plsql_entry_object_id, a.plsql_entry_subprogram_id, a.plsql_object_id, a.plsql_subprogram_id, a.service_hash, a.qc_session_id, a.qc_instance_id, a.qc_session_serial#, nvl(a.event, CASE WHEN bitand(a.time_model, power(2, 19)) > 0 THEN 'CPU: IM Populate' WHEN bitand(a.time_model, power(2, 20)) > 0 THEN 'CPU: IM Prepopulate' WHEN bitand(a.time_model, power(2, 21)) > 0 THEN 'CPU: IM Repopulate' WHEN bitand(a.time_model, power(2, 22)) > 0 THEN 'CPU: IM Trickle Repop' WHEN bitand(a.time_model, power(2, 18)) > 0 THEN 'CPU: IM Query' ELSE 'CPU + Wait for CPU' END) as event, nvl(a.event_id, 1) as event_id, nvl(a.wait_class, 'CPU') as wait_class, nvl(a.wait_class_id, 9999) as wait_class_id, a.seq#, a.p1, a.p1text, a.p2, a.p2text, a.p3, a.p3text, a.wait_time, a.time_waited, a.xid, a.blocking_session, a.blocking_session_serial#, a.blocking_session_status, a.blocking_inst_id, a.current_obj#, a.current_file#, a.current_block#, a.top_level_call#, a.top_level_call_name, a.spid, a.stid, a.program, a.module, a.action, a.client_id, a.client_info, a.remote_instance#, a.in_connection_mgmt, a.in_parse, a.in_hard_parse, a.in_sql_execution, a.in_plsql_execution, a.in_plsql_rpc, a.in_plsql_compilation, a.in_java_execution, a.in_bind, a.in_cursor_close, a.in_sequence_load FROM AWR_PDB_ACTIVE_SESS_HISTORY a, (SELECT /*+ NO_MERGE */ dbid, con_dbid, substr(x , 20) as pdb_name, instance_number FROM ( SELECT i.dbid, i.con_dbid, i.instance_number, max(to_char(i.OPEN_TIME, 'YYYY:MM:DD HH24:MI:SS') || i.PDB_NAME) as x FROM AWR_PDB_pdb_instance i WHERE i.con_dbid <> 0 GROUP BY i.dbid, i.con_dbid, i.instance_number)) p WHERE 1=1 AND a.con_dbid = p.con_dbid(+) AND a.dbid = p.dbid(+) AND a.instance_number = p.instance_number(+)) ash WHERE ash.dbid = :dbid AND ash.snap_id > :bid AND ash.snap_id <= :eid AND ash.instance_number = :inst_num ) a WHERE 1 = 1 and blocking_session_status = 'VALID' GROUP BY blocking_session, blocking_session_serial#, blocking_inst_id , event ) d12gb_ash ) d12aa_ash WHERE d12aa_ash.dim1_rank <= 5 AND d12aa_ash.dim1_percentage >= 1 AND d12aa_ash.dim12_rank <= 3 AND ( (d12aa_ash.dim12_rank <= 1 ) OR ( (d12aa_ash.dim12_rank > 1 ) AND (d12aa_ash.dim12_percentage >= 1 ) ) ) ORDER BY d12aa_ash.dim1_rank, d12aa_ash.dim12_rank ) ash WHERE 1 = 1 ORDER BY ash.dim1_rank, ash.dim12_rank

1mdy0zx139b9gWITH MSGS AS ( SELECT ID, JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_group' VALUE MSG_ARRAY, 'msg_text' VALUE SYS.DBMS_ADVISOR.FORMAT_MESSAGE_GROUP(ID) RETURNING CLOB) AS MSG FROM ( SELECT ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_id' VALUE (FAC || '-' || LPAD(TO_CHAR(MESSAGE#), 5, '0')), 'seq' VALUE SEQ, 'p1' VALUE P1, 'p2' VALUE P2, 'p3' VALUE P3, 'p4' VALUE P4, 'p5' VALUE P5 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) ORDER BY SEQ RETURNING CLOB) MSG_ARRAY FROM SYS.WRI$_ADV_MESSAGE_GROUPS WHERE TASK_ID = :B1 GROUP BY ID ) ) , FDG_BREAK AS ( SELECT FINDING_ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT( 'instance_number' VALUE INSTANCE_NUMBER, 'database_time' VALUE DATABASE_TIME, 'active_sessions' VALUE ACTIVE_SESSIONS, 'perc_active_sessions' VALUE PERC_ACTIVE_SESSIONS RETURNING CLOB) ORDER BY PERC_ACTIVE_SESSIONS DESC, INSTANCE_NUMBER ASC RETURNING CLOB) AS BREAKDOWN FROM SYS.DBA_ADDM_FDG_BREAKDOWN WHERE TASK_ID = :B1 GROUP BY FINDING_ID ) , OBJS AS ( SELECT OBJECT_ID, CASE WHEN TYPE = 'SQL' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'sql_id' VALUE ATTR1, 'plan_hash' VALUE ATTR2, 'sql_text' VALUE ATTR4, 'con_dbid' VALUE ATTR10 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'DATABASE OBJECT' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'object_id' VALUE ATTR1, 'con_dbid' VALUE ATTR2 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'DATABASE BLOCK' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'object_id' VALUE ATTR3, 'file_number' VALUE ATTR2, 'block_number' VALUE ATTR1 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'DATABASE FILE' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'file_name' VALUE ATTR1 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'TABLESPACE' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'tablespace_id' VALUE ATTR1, 'name' VALUE ATTR2 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'DATABASE LATCH' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'name' VALUE ATTR1 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) WHEN TYPE = 'DATABASE ENQ' THEN JSON_OBJECT ( 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'code' VALUE ATTR1, 'id1' VALUE ATTR2, 'id2' VALUE ATTR3 ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) END AS OBJ FROM SYS.DBA_ADVISOR_OBJECTS WHERE TASK_ID = :B1 AND TYPE IN ('SQL', 'DATABASE OBJECT', 'DATABASE BLOCK', 'DATABASE FILE', 'TABLESPACE', 'DATABASE LATCH', 'DATABASE ENQ') ) , BASE_ACTS AS ( SELECT D.REC_ID, A.ID AS ACTION_ID, JSON_OBJECT( 'action_id' VALUE A.ID, 'object' VALUE (SELECT O.OBJ FROM OBJS O WHERE O.OBJECT_ID = A.OBJ_ID), 'command' VALUE C.COMMAND_NAME, 'message' VALUE (SELECT M.MSG FROM MSGS M WHERE M.ID = A.MSG_ID), 'filtered' VALUE A.FILTERED, 'result_status' VALUE DECODE(A.RES_STATUS#, 0, 'AUTOMATION PENDING', 1, 'TIMED OUT', 2, 'MISSING INFORMATION', 3, 'COMPLETED WITH NO RESULTS', 4, 'COMPLETED AND IMPLEMENTED', 5, 'ENCOUNTERED ERROR', NULL), 'result_message' VALUE (SELECT R.MSG FROM MSGS R WHERE R.ID = A.RES_MSG_ID) ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) AS ACTION FROM SYS.WRI$_ADV_ACTIONS A, SYS.WRI$_ADV_REC_ACTIONS D, SYS.DBA_ADVISOR_COMMANDS C WHERE A.TASK_ID = :B1 AND D.TASK_ID = :B1 AND D.ACT_ID = A.ID AND A.COMMAND = C.COMMAND_ID ) , ACTS AS ( SELECT REC_ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(ACTION ORDER BY ACTION_ID RETURNING CLOB) AS ACTIONS FROM BASE_ACTS GROUP BY REC_ID ) , BASE_RTNS AS ( SELECT A.REC_ID, A.ID AS RATIONALE_ID, JSON_OBJECT( 'rationale_id' VALUE A.ID, 'object' VALUE (SELECT O.OBJ FROM OBJS O WHERE O.OBJECT_ID = A.OBJ_ID), 'message' VALUE (SELECT M.MSG FROM MSGS M WHERE M.ID = A.MSG_ID) ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) AS RATIONALE FROM SYS.WRI$_ADV_RATIONALE A WHERE A.TASK_ID = :B1 ) , RTNS AS ( SELECT REC_ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(RATIONALE ORDER BY RATIONALE_ID RETURNING CLOB) AS RTNS FROM BASE_RTNS GROUP BY REC_ID ) , BASE_RECS AS ( SELECT R.FINDING_ID, R.REC_ID, R.RANK, R.TYPE, R.BENEFIT, R.FILTERED, DECODE(TYPE, 'Application Analysis', 'SMG-00646', 'DB Configuration', 'SMG-00647', 'Host Configuration', 'SMG-00648', 'SQL Tuning', 'SMG-00649', 'Segment Tuning', 'SMG-00650', 'Schema', 'SMG-00651', 'Examine instance ADDM', 'SMG-00686' , 'Exadata Configuration', 'SMG-00698' , 'SMG-00652') AS TYPE_MSG, (SELECT A.ACTIONS FROM ACTS A WHERE A.REC_ID = R.REC_ID) AS ACTIONS, (SELECT A.RTNS FROM RTNS A WHERE A.REC_ID = R.REC_ID) AS RATIONALES FROM SYS.DBA_ADVISOR_RECOMMENDATIONS R WHERE R.TASK_ID = :B1 ) , RECS AS ( SELECT FINDING_ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT( 'rec_id' VALUE REC_ID, 'rank' VALUE RANK, 'type' VALUE TYPE, 'type_message' VALUE JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_group' VALUE JSON_ARRAY(JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_id' VALUE TYPE_MSG, 'seq' VALUE 0 )), 'msg_text' VALUE SYS.DBMS_ADVISOR.FORMAT_MESSAGE(TYPE_MSG) ), 'benefit' VALUE BENEFIT, 'actions' VALUE ACTIONS, 'rationales' VALUE RATIONALES, 'filtered' VALUE FILTERED ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB) ORDER BY BENEFIT DESC, RANK ASC, REC_ID ASC RETURNING CLOB) AS RECOMMENDATIONS FROM BASE_RECS GROUP BY FINDING_ID ) , REC_TYPES AS ( SELECT FINDING_ID, JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT( 'type_message' VALUE JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_group' VALUE JSON_ARRAY(JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_id' VALUE TYPE_MSG, 'seq' VALUE 0 )), 'msg_text' VALUE SYS.DBMS_ADVISOR.FORMAT_MESSAGE(TYPE_MSG) ), 'count' VALUE CNT RETURNING CLOB) ORDER BY CNT DESC, TYPE_MSG ASC RETURNING CLOB) AS RTYPES FROM (SELECT FINDING_ID, TYPE_MSG, COUNT(*) AS CNT FROM BASE_RECS WHERE NVL(FILTERED, 'N') = 'N' GROUP BY FINDING_ID, TYPE_MSG) GROUP BY FINDING_ID ) , BASE_FINDINGS AS ( SELECT A.FINDING_ID, CASE WHEN (F.NAME_MSG_CODE IS NULL OR LENGTH(F.NAME_MSG_CODE) < 5) THEN NULL ELSE JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_group' VALUE JSON_ARRAY(JSON_OBJECT( 'msg_id' VALUE F.NAME_MSG_CODE, 'seq' VALUE 0 )), 'msg_text' VALUE SYS.DBMS_ADVISOR.FORMAT_MESSAGE(F.NAME_MSG_CODE) ) END AS F.MSG_ID) AS MESSAGE, F.MORE_INFO_ID, F.FILTERED, A.DATABASE_TIME, A.ACTIVE_SESSIONS, A.PERC_ACTIVE_SESS, A.IS_AGGREGATE FROM SYS.WRI$_ADV_ADDM_FDG A, SYS.WRI$_ADV_FINDINGS F WHERE F.ID = A.FINDING_ID AND F.TASK_ID = :B1 AND A.TASK_ID = :B1 ) , FDGS_JSON AS ( SELECT F.FINDING_ID, F.FINDING_TYPE, F.PARENT, F.IMPACT, F.MESSAGE, JSON_OBJECT( 'id' VALUE F.FINDING_ID, 'is_parent_of' VALUE TO_NUMBER( F.ACTIVE_SESSIONS, 'perc_active_sess' VALUE F.PERC_ACTIVE_SESS, 'is_aggregate' VALUE F.IS_AGGREGATE, 'instance_breakdown' VALUE (SELECT B.BREAKDOWN FROM FDG_BREAK B WHERE B.FINDING_ID = F.FINDING_ID), 'recommendations' VALUE (SELECT R.RECOMMENDATIONS FROM RECS R WHERE R.FINDING_ID = F.FINDING_ID), 'rec_types' VALUE (SELECT T.RTYPES FROM REC_TYPES T WHERE T.FINDING_ID = F.FINDING_ID) ABSENT ON NULL RETURNING CLOB ) AS FINDING FROM BASE_FINDINGS F ) , FINDINGS AS ( SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(FINDING ORDER BY IMPACT DESC, FINDING_ID ASC RETURNING CLOB) X FROM FDGS_JSON WHERE FINDING_TYPE IN ('PROBLEM', 'SYMPTOM') AND PARENT = 0 ) , PATH_FINDINGS AS ( SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(FINDING ORDER BY IMPACT DESC, FINDING_ID ASC RETURNING CLOB) X FROM FDGS_JSON WHERE FINDING_TYPE IN ('PROBLEM', 'SYMPTOM') AND PARENT > 0 ) , INFO_FINDINGS AS ( SELECT FINDING_TYPE, JSON_ARRAYAGG(MESSAGE ORDER BY FINDING_ID RETURNING CLOB) X FROM FDGS_JSON WHERE NOT FINDING_TYPE IN ('PROBLEM', 'SYMPTOM') GROUP BY FINDING_TYPE ) , INST AS ( SELECT JSON_ARRAYAGG(JSON_OBJECT( 'local_task_id' VALUE LOCAL_TASK_ID, 'instance_number' VALUE INSTANCE_NUMBER, 'instance_name' VALUE INSTANCE_NAME, 'host_name' VALUE HOST_NAME, 'status' b.total_waits total_waits, e.time_waited - b.time_waited time_waited from AWR_PDB_service_wait_class b, AWR_PDB_service_wait_class e where b.snap_id = :bid and e.snap_id = :eid and b.instance_number = :inst_num and e.instance_number = :inst_num and b.dbid = :dbid and b.con_dbid = e.con_dbid and e.dbid = :dbid and e.con_dbid member of :con_dbid_list and b.wait_class_id = e.wait_class_id and b.service_name_hash = e.service_name_hash) s2 group by s2.service_name) stat2 where stat1.service_name = stat2.service_name order by db_time desc, service_name) where rownum <= :top_n_svc
d3fmdy3akqsbjselect service_name, db_time / 1000000, db_cpu / 1000000, phy_reads / 1000, log_reads / 1000 from (select s1.service_name, sum(decode(s1.stat_name, 'DB time', s1.diff, 0)) db_time, sum(decode(s1.stat_name, 'DB CPU', s1.diff, 0)) db_cpu, sum(decode(s1.stat_name, 'physical reads', s1.diff, 0)) phy_reads, sum(decode(s1.stat_name, 'session logical reads', s1.diff, 0)) log_reads from (select e.service_name service_name, e.stat_name stat_name, e.value - b.value diff from AWR_PDB_service_stat b, AWR_PDB_service_stat e where b.snap_id = :bid and e.snap_id = :eid and b.instance_number = :inst_num and e.instance_number = :inst_num and b.dbid = :dbid and e.dbid = :dbid and b.con_dbid = e.con_dbid and e.con_dbid member of :con_dbid_list and b.stat_id = e.stat_id and b.service_name_hash = e.service_name_hash) s1 group by s1.service_name order by db_time desc, service_name) where rownum <= :top_n_svc
d3kxxfctxz8mfselect e.stat_name key, sum(e.value) - nvl(sum(b.value), 0) value from AWR_PDB_SYSSTAT e, AWR_PDB_SYSSTAT b where e.dbid = :dbid and e.snap_id = :eid and e.instance_number MEMBER OF :inst_num_list and e.con_dbid member of :con_dbid_list and e.stat_name MEMBER OF :name_list and b.dbid (+) = e.dbid and b.snap_id (+) = :bid and b.instance_number (+) = e.instance_number and b.con_dbid (+) = e.con_dbid and b.stat_name (+) = e.stat_name group by e.stat_name

Undo Segment Summary

  • Min/Max TR (mins) - Min and Max Tuned Retention (minutes)

  • uS - unexpired Stolen, uR - unexpired Released, uU - unexpired reUsed
  • eS - expired Stolen, eR - expired Released, eU - expired reUsed

Active Session History (ASH) Report

  • Top SQL with Top Events
  • Top SQL with Top Row Sources
  • Top Sessions
  • Top Blocking Sessions
  • Top PL/SQL Procedures
  • Top Events
  • Top Event P1/P2/P3 Values
  • Top DB Objects
  • Activity Over Time
Back to Top

Top SQL with Top Events

  • Top SQL statements by DB Time along with the top events by DB Time for those SQLs.
  • % Activity is the percentage of DB Time due to the SQL.
  • % Event is the percentage of DB Time due to the event that the SQL is waiting on.
  • % Row Source is the percentage of DB Time due to the row source for the SQL waiting on the event.
  • Executions is the number of executions of the SQL that were sampled in ASH.
SQL IDPlan HashExecutions% ActivityEvent% EventTop Row Source% Row SourceSQL TextContainer Name
2651cv0dd1yb9138873495312.50 CPU + Wait for CPU2.50SELECT STATEMENT2.50 SELECT DBMS_WORKLOAD_REPOSITOR...CDB$ROOT
5yv7yvjgjxugg 02.50 CPU + Wait for CPU2.50SELECT STATEMENT2.50 select TIME_WAITED_MICRO from ...CDB$ROOT

Back to Active Session History (ASH) Report
Back to Top

Top SQL with Top Row Sources

  • Top SQL statements by DB Time along with the top row sources by DB Time for those SQLs.
  • % Activity is the percentage of DB Time due to the SQL.
  • % Row Source is the percentage of DB Time spent on the row source by that SQL.
  • % Event is the percentage of DB Time spent on the event by the SQL executing the row source.
  • Executions is the number of executions of the SQL that were sampled in ASH.
SQL IDPlan HashExecutions% ActivityRow Source% Row SourceTop Event% EventSQL TextContainer Name

Top Sessions

  • '# Samples Active' shows the number of ASH samples in which the session was found waiting for that particular event. The percentage shown in this column is calculated with respect to wall clock time and not total database activity.
  • 'XIDs' shows the number of distinct transaction IDs sampled in ASH when the session was waiting for that particular event

Top Blocking Sessions

  • Blocking session activity percentages are calculated with respect to waits on enqueues, latches and "buffer busy" only
  • '% Activity' represents the load on the database caused by a particular blocking session
  • '# Samples Active' shows the number of ASH samples in which the blocking session was found active.
  • 'XIDs' shows the number of distinct transaction IDs sampled in ASH when the blocking session was found active.
Blocking Sid (Inst)% ActivityEvent Caused% EventUserProgram# Samples ActiveXIDs
126,23228( 1)2.50latch free2.50 SYSoracle@mk23ai (SMCO)1/137 [ 1%]0

Top PL/SQL Procedures

  • 'PL/SQL entry subprogram' represents the application's top-level entry-point (procedure, function, trigger, package initialization or RPC call) into PL/SQL.
  • 'PL/SQL current subprogram' is the PL/SQL subprogram being executed at the point of sampling . If the value is 'SQL', it represents the percentage of time spent executing SQL for the particular PL/SQL entry subprogram
PL/SQL Entry Subprogram% ActivityPL/SQL Current Subprogram% CurrentContainer Name

Activity Over Time

  • Analysis period is divided into smaller time slots as indicated in the 'Slot Time (Duration)'.
  • Top 3 events are reported in each of those slots
  • 'Slot Count' shows the number of ASH samples in that slot
  • 'Event Count' shows the number of ASH samples waiting for that event in that slot
  • '% Event' is 'Event Count' over all ASH samples in the analysis period
